### bug (207)

- **R3-0083** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/audit-signer/src/custody.rs:144` — The anchor key is written non-atomically, so the race the code claims to handle (and a crash mid-mint) ends in a permanent Corrupt refusal *Fix:* Write the seed to a temporary file and publish it with an exclusive link/rename so a reader never sees a partial key.
- **R3-0084** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/audit-signer/src/custody.rs:297` — save_state renames without syncing the directory, while decide() relies on the mark being on disk before replying *Fix:* fsync the parent directory after the rename (MOVEFILE_WRITE_THROUGH on Windows).
- **R3-0085** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/audit-signer/src/register.rs:113` — install_plan() emits the peer-allowlist step AFTER `sc.exe start`, the reverse of what apply() and the module doc require *Fix:* Insert the additional steps before the start step, and assert the order in the_install_plan_names_the_peer_allowlist test.
- **R3-0086** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/audit-signer/src/register.rs:311` — The ledger DACL plan is built and thrown away; nothing ever applies it, though verify_installed reads it back *Fix:* Apply the ledger plan to paths.ledger_dir in apply() and read it back, or remove the plan from apply() and say the ledger DACL is unapplied.
- **R3-0087** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/chain_executor.rs:1026` — `process_group_id` reported to the supervisor is the invoker's pid, not a process group *Fix:* Spawn with process_group(0) and report that id, or rename the field to what is actually sent.
- **R3-0088** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/chain_executor.rs:1033` — A refused `execution-started` kills only the sudo invoker, not the recorder/launcher/executor it started *Fix:* Spawn the recorder in its own process group and signal the group through a path the recorder uid honours, or have the supervisor's refusal stop the a…
- **R3-0089** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/chain_hops.rs:37` — A principal's typed refusal reason is parsed and then discarded without being logged *Fix:* Log the principal and reason on stderr in hop_request before collapsing to UpstreamBlocked.
- **R3-0090** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/main.rs:345` — Malformed or oversized `uids` entries are silently dropped or truncated before the TCB floor, in a block that claims to fail closed in every direction *Fix:* Return fail_closed() when `uids` is absent, or any entry is not an integer that fits u32.
- **R3-0091** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/manifest_resolver.rs:399` — The custody observation is never cleared, so a turn that fails after an earlier success leaves the earlier observation in place *Fix:* Set the cell to None at the top of resolve_keys (before any check) and add a test that succeeds once, then fails a persist, and asserts NoTrustedMani…
- **R3-0092** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/manifest_resolver.rs:399` — A poisoned custody lock is swallowed and resolve_keys still returns Ok *Fix:* Map the lock error to TurnReason::UpstreamBlocked, as the floor lock does.
- **R3-0093** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/preflight.rs:713` — Preflight's uid_set truncates and drops uids the way the fixed parser refuses *Fix:* Report a non-uid value in the uids block as NOT MET instead of skipping or truncating it.
- **R3-0094** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/preflight.rs:714` — Config uids are narrowed with `as u32`, so an out-of-range or negative value silently becomes another uid *Fix:* Use u32::try_from and report NOT MET (and refuse in main.rs) on a value that does not fit.
- **R3-0095** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/preflight.rs:755` — `seven_distinct_accounts` counts distinct numbers, so a collapsed pair is hidden by one extra entry in `uids` *Fix:* Look up the seven named roles explicitly and require their uids to be pairwise distinct.
- **R3-0096** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/preflight.rs:825` — The launcher row takes the FIRST pinned `privileged-launcher.bin`, where the anchor row refuses two *Fix:* Use pin.sole_artifact("privileged-launcher.bin") and report AmbiguousRole as NOT MET.
- **R3-0097** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/preflight.rs:987` — The preflight has no row for two refusals the broker's floor makes: pin-manifest custody and broker self-identity *Fix:* Add a custody row using manifest_custody_violation over Host::stat and a self-identity row over the two BROKER_SELF_ROLES entries, or name both in th…
- **R3-0098** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/preflight.rs:1111` — The floor row reports MET on the file's owner and mode only, though the persist is temp-file + rename in the directory *Fix:* Stat the floor's parent directory and require the broker uid to own and be able to write it, as check_ledger already does for the database directory.
- **R3-0099** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/preflight.rs:1152` — `sockets.authority` is MET for any path that exists, socket or not *Fix:* Require `mode & 0o170000 == 0o140000` (S_IFSOCK) before reporting MET.
- **R3-0100** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/preflight.rs:1255` — The `resolved.identifiers` row reports NOT MET for run_id, task_id and requested_at_ms, which the broker never uses *Fix:* Reduce the row to workspace_id and install_id (the two the ladder really takes from config) and correct its `what`/`refusal` text.
- **R3-0101** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/schema/0017_conversation_participants.sql:13` — added_at defaults to datetime('now') ('YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS') while the only writer stores epoch-ms text *Fix:* Drop the default (the writer always supplies the value) or make it an epoch-ms expression.
- **R3-0102** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/agent_bundle.rs:174` — GRANT_SCHEMA is 2 and 'a v1 and a v2 grant are different objects', but verify() never checks grant.schema, flow.schema or any artifact_type *Fix:* Refuse in verify() unless grant.schema == GRANT_SCHEMA, flow.schema == 1 and each artifact_type equals its constant.
- **R3-0103** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/agent_bundle.rs:534` — max_steps is a bound the flow file sets on itself; no runtime ceiling exists *Fix:* Compare against a compiled constant (the 8 that build() writes) rather than the file's own field.
- **R3-0104** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/agent_bundle.rs:565` — build() returns Ok(digest) for bundles verify() always refuses (one step, or more than the hard-coded max_steps 8) *Fix:* Have build() apply the same step-count rule (or call verify on what it wrote) and return the refusal.
- **R3-0105** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/bin/ladder_output_pull.rs:519` — served_chunk_sha256 is the digest of the base64 text, not of the bytes it is documented as *Fix:* Decode the base64url before hashing, or rename the field served_chunk_b64_sha256.
- **R3-0106** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/bin/produce_agent_artifact.rs:171` — An unparseable `touched` column is exported as null instead of failing the evidence export *Fix:* Propagate the parse error with `?`.
- **R3-0107** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/broker_orchestrator.rs:138` — settle() failures are discarded, so a committed message can leave its broker_turns row 'live' and later be recorded as 'blocked' *Fix:* Surface a failed settle (log/audit it and reconcile from the committed message) instead of `let _`, and never let expiry overwrite a turn whose messa…
- **R3-0108** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/broker_turns.rs:412` — An empty receipt_id or nonce is reported as a replay *Fix:* Reject empty ids before the INSERT with LedgerRefusal::Fault (or a dedicated variant) and assert the exact refusal in the test.
- **R3-0109** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/credentials.rs:138` — unbind records a 'credential.unbound' audit event even when no binding existed *Fix:* Record the event only when the DELETE affected a row, or record a distinct no-op event.
- **R3-0110** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/db.rs:33` — A database migrated by a newer build opens without refusal; SCHEMA_VERSION and current_version are read only by tests *Fix:* In open(), refuse when current_version(conn) > SCHEMA_VERSION.
- **R3-0111** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/governed_message_store.rs:48` — A broker database created under the old CHECK refuses every demonstration_custody commit, with no migration *Fix:* Detect the old constraint in create_schema and rebuild the table, or refuse at startup with a message naming it.
- **R3-0112** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/governed_message_store.rs:94` — The membership half of verify_readback cannot fail from its only caller and no test reaches it *Fix:* Add a test calling verify_readback("unverified", ..., "unverified") or drop the membership clause and its 'Both halves matter' comment.
- **R3-0113** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/governed_output_pull.rs:396` — The refused arm of parse_read_reply does not check the other four keys the frame must carry *Fix:* On the refused arm require output_stream_id, seq, bytes_b64 and eof to be present, with bytes_b64 and eof null.
- **R3-0114** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/governed_prepare.rs:162` — PrepareError's machine name and field are discarded by the only production caller, so a bad BROPS_GOVERNED_* override is undiagnosable *Fix:* Log the PrepareError (Display) on the broker's stderr before mapping it to UpstreamBlocked.
- **R3-0115** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/governed_sidecar.rs:401` — NUL-byte refusal message contains two runs of ~22 literal spaces *Fix:* Re-wrap the literal with `\` line continuations like its neighbours.
- **R3-0116** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/governed_sidecar.rs:776` — Spawn-failure remediation names environment variables the broker never reads *Fix:* Emit that sentence only when `principal` is None; on the distinct arm point at the config's `sidecar` block and the sudoers vector.
- **R3-0117** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/governed_verification.rs:558` — Four comparisons in bind_attested_evidence can never fail when reached, and the run-identity tests cannot tell them from step 4b *Fix:* Say in the comment that (d) and the first half of (e) are implied, or drop them and keep one tested copy.
- **R3-0118** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/key_manifest.rs:433` — A directory-sync failure reports NotPersisted after the floor file has already been replaced *Fix:* Document the outcome as 'written, durability unknown' or return the advanced floor with a distinct error.
- **R3-0119** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/lib.rs:62` — now() swallows a clock error into "0", breaking the fixed-width ordering its doc promises *Fix:* Return a Result (or zero-pad and refuse a pre-epoch clock) instead of defaulting to 0.
- **R3-0120** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/local_write_record.rs:352` — check_chain reports Intact for a row whose operation/subject_kind is outside the domain when the original token equals map()'s default *Fix:* Give check_chain the same raw-token domain check record_break makes, or make map() return an error for an unknown token.
- **R3-0121** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/receipt.rs:382` — BadSignatureLength reports base64 length as signature bytes *Fix:* Return a distinct oversize-wire error for the pre-decode cap.
- **R3-0122** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/receipt.rs:651` — TooLarge reports the base64 character count as the envelope's byte size *Fix:* Use a separate variant or message for the pre-decode length guard.
- **R3-0123** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/receipt.rs:666` — Duplicate-key detection is in-band text matching on the serde error; other errors containing '__dup__' are misclassified *Fix:* Carry the duplicate key out of the visitor through a side channel (e.g. a Cell/thread-local or a custom error type) instead of parsing the message.
- **R3-0124** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/receipt.rs:706` — Timestamp validation accepts non-canonical integers ('+2000', '0002000') *Fix:* Require the string to equal its parsed value's to_string() (digits only, no sign, no leading zero).
- **R3-0125** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/receipt_store.rs:345` — MAX_REASON_BYTES is not applied to verification errors; attacker-sized strings reach the evidence row and the UI *Fix:* Apply bounded_reason inside insert_attempt/record (and record_pre_verification_block) rather than trusting each caller.
- **R3-0126** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/receipt_store.rs:798` — Nested-transaction refusal names verify_and_record_receipt whichever entry point was called *Fix:* Pass the caller's name into in_immediate_tx or word the message generically.
- **R3-0127** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/repo.rs:871` — approve_confirmed reports every SQLite error as "not found" *Fix:* Map only QueryReturnedNoRows to NotFound and pass other errors through as CoreError::Sqlite.
- **R3-0128** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/repo.rs:1716` — knowledge::search is documented case-insensitive but SQLite LIKE folds case for ASCII only *Fix:* Narrow the doc to ASCII, or lowercase both sides in Rust before matching.
- **R3-0129** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/repo.rs:1850` — memory::set_pinned writes no audit event, unlike create and delete *Fix:* Take an Actor and record `memory.pinned` in the same transaction.
- **R3-0130** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/repo.rs:2197` — A failed step execution, which has already spent a native-confirmed grant, writes no audit event *Fix:* Take an Actor and record a `run_step.failed` audit event in the same transaction.
- **R3-0131** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/repo.rs:2315` — set_step_status consumes an approval grant and completes a gated step with no audit record *Fix:* Give set_step_status an Actor and record a `run_step.status_changed` event in the transaction.
- **R3-0132** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/repo.rs:2452` — advance() stamps a run terminal while another step is still `active` *Fix:* Before stamping a terminal status, check that no step is still `active` and return without terminating if one is.
- **R3-0133** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/repo.rs:2691` — automations::run commits the action before, and separately from, the run-log row that marks it as run *Fix:* Run execute_action and the automation_runs insert inside one atomic() call.
- **R3-0134** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/repo.rs:2725` — parse_interval_ms panics on a trigger ending in a multi-byte character, inside the scheduler tick holding the DB mutex *Fix:* Split on the last char (`rest.chars().last()` / `char_indices`) instead of `len() - 1`, and add a non-ASCII test.
- **R3-0135** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/repo.rs:2731` — parse_interval_ms multiplies without an overflow check *Fix:* Use checked_mul and return None on overflow.
- **R3-0136** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/repo.rs:2778` — A failing automations::list ends the tick before the agent half, contradicting the doc that both halves are always attempted *Fix:* Capture the list error into first_error and fall through to the agent half.
- **R3-0137** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/repo.rs:3064` — enqueue_due slices `&digest[..12]` on an unvalidated digest *Fix:* Validate the digest as 64 lowercase hex characters in register/set_active, or use `digest.get(..12)`.
- **R3-0138** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/repo.rs:3067` — A dropped duplicate flow run is still audited and counted as enqueued *Fix:* Put bundle_id in the run id and count/audit only when the INSERT changed a row.
- **R3-0139** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/repo.rs:3094` — scheduler_ticks gets one row per minute and nothing ever prunes it *Fix:* Trim rows older than a fixed window inside record_tick.
- **R3-0140** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/repo.rs:3140` — claim_and_run dispatches queued runs of a bundle that was disarmed *Fix:* Have claim_and_run require a matching agent_bundle_active row (or have disarm settle the bundle's queued runs as refused).
- **R3-0141** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/repo.rs:3201` — run_steps executes steps in array order and never reads the flow's `next` edges *Fix:* Walk the flow by following `next` from the first step (refusing a malformed graph), or refuse at verify any flow whose edges are not the array order.
- **R3-0142** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/repo.rs:3204` — A `store` step ignores its `verb` and always writes a knowledge note *Fix:* Match on the verb against a closed set and refuse an unknown one with a typed Refusal.
- **R3-0143** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/repo.rs:4528` — security::summary counts escalated approvals as neither pending nor decided *Fix:* Count `escalated` with pending, or add a third field for it.
- **R3-0144** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/repo.rs:4759` — The five seeded pending approvals can never be approved: they carry no nonce or request digest *Fix:* Seed these through approvals::create, or mark them as illustrative so the UI does not offer Approve on them.
- **R3-0145** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/supervisor_ledger.rs:808` — enqueue_terminal answers Idempotent on payload hash alone and takes run_id from the caller *Fix:* Compare every stored column on retry and read run_id from governed_turn_acceptance (the function is currently unreached).
- **R3-0146** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/supervisor_ledger.rs:995` — The stored-row corruption check accepts a 64-character non-hex final_event_hash *Fix:* Apply the same hex test in refuse_corrupt that validate applies to the presented head.
- **R3-0147** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/launcher/src/main.rs:1028` — `drop_bounding_and_ambient_caps` ignores every PR_CAPBSET_DROP result but the step is journalled as completed *Fix:* Treat any errno other than EINVAL from PR_CAPBSET_DROP as failure and fix the comment to name the /proc/self/status check.
- **R3-0148** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/proof/src/bin/governed_recorder.rs:642` — Head-sequence counter silently restarts at 1 on any open error where `path.exists()` is false *Fix:* Decide 'missing' from the open's own errno (ENOENT only) instead of a second path lookup.
- **R3-0149** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/proof/src/bin/governed_recorder.rs:828` — `waitpid` result is unchecked; a failed wait reads as exit code 0 *Fix:* Check `waitpid(..) == pid` (retrying EINTR) and treat any other return as a refusal.
- **R3-0150** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/proof/src/bin/ladder_turn.rs:218` — Evidence writers swallow every I/O error, so a run can exit 0 with no evidence on disk *Fix:* Return the write result and make `finish` exit non-zero when its own evidence document could not be written.
- **R3-0151** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/proof/src/bin/ladder_turn.rs:780` — `manifest_sig_unreadable` reports the manifest path and hides a missing config key *Fix:* Split the lookup from the read, return `config_missing`/`trust.manifest_sig_path` for the first and pass the signature path as the detail for the sec…
- **R3-0152** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/provision/src/anchor.rs:592` — A read-only filesystem makes every custody probe 'unmeasurable', so the app refuses on the least writable anchor *Fix:* Treat ReadOnlyFilesystem as a refusal of the write (recorded as such) rather than as unmeasurable.
- **R3-0153** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/provision/src/lib.rs:1033` — Argument-validation failures are reported as a corrupt trust store *Fix:* Add an InvalidArgument variant for caller input and keep Corrupt for on-disk state.
- **R3-0154** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/provision/src/lib.rs:1436` — The idempotency and occupancy checks use is_file()/exists(), which answer false for every error — the defect this file fixed for the custody record *Fix:* Use symlink_metadata and classify only NotFound as absent, as custody_record_from_read and settle_existing do.
- **R3-0155** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/provision/src/lib.rs:2043` — The 'keys directory holds the retained authorities and nothing else' check accepts extension-less names and directories *Fix:* Require the name to end in .json, be a regular file, and be exactly one of the manifest-covered key files.
- **R3-0156** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/provision/src/lib.rs:2169` — verify_store never reads the anti-rollback floor file it hands to the engine *Fix:* Read the floor in verify_store and require it to equal the registry payload's registry_version.
- **R3-0157** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/provision/src/posix_install.rs:240` — refuse_ambiguous_path accepts repeated separators, so the installer binds a spelling the application never computes *Fix:* Refuse any path whose string differs from its component-rebuilt form.
- **R3-0158** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/provision/src/posix_install.rs:247` — refuse_ambiguous_path can never refuse a `.` component: Path::components() normalises it away on an absolute path *Fix:* Check the raw string (split on '/' and refuse any "." or empty segment) instead of relying on components().
- **R3-0159** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/provision/tests/posix_install.rs:796` — Desktop-account fallback skips $SUDO_USER when BROPS_TEST_DESKTOP_USER is set but empty or 'root' *Fix:* Apply the non-empty/non-root filter to each source before or_else, so a rejected first value falls through to $SUDO_USER.
- **R3-0160** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/ai.rs:599` — claude-cli readiness is `--version` succeeding, but the status text speaks about login *Fix:* Word the detail as "binary found (login unverified)", as the Anthropic arm does for its key.
- **R3-0161** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/ai.rs:638` — The Ollama status discards the validation error and always blames a non-local host *Fix:* Keep the Err string from validate_ollama_url and put it in the detail.
- **R3-0162** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/ai.rs:732` — Stop does nothing for the Anthropic and Ollama providers *Fix:* Race the HTTP future against the cancel flag (poll it as the CLI loop does) and return the cancelled result.
- **R3-0163** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/ai.rs:1419` — Every agent turn creates an empty quarantine directory even when nothing changed *Fix:* Create the quarantine directory lazily, on the first file that needs it.
- **R3-0164** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/ai.rs:1487` — The guard restores bytes but not the file mode, and does not detect a mode change *Fix:* Record the unix mode in FileSnapshot, compare it, and re-apply it on restore.
- **R3-0165** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/ai.rs:2179` — clean_path_token strips in the wrong order, so a backticked path followed by a period is rejected *Fix:* Trim trailing punctuation first, then the quote characters.
- **R3-0166** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/ai.rs:2235` — A Markdown-bold label (`**scope:** path`) yields no scope *Fix:* Strip leading emphasis characters from the remainder after the label as well.
- **R3-0167** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/ai.rs:2255` — One prose token on a later scope line erases paths already taken from earlier lines *Fix:* Collect each line into a local vector and append it to the target only if every token passed.
- **R3-0168** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/ai.rs:2533` — Stop settles the trust surface before the killed child has exited *Fix:* Await child.wait() (bounded by a short timeout) before calling settle on the cancel path.
- **R3-0169** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/ai.rs:2786` — The non-streaming CLI path gives an agent-mode turn 120 s while the streaming path gives it 900 s *Fix:* Use the same agent-aware deadline in claude_cli.
- **R3-0170** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/ai.rs:3205` — A base URL with a trailing slash passes validation and then produces a double-slash endpoint *Fix:* Trim a trailing '/' from the URL before appending the endpoint.
- **R3-0171** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands.rs:1408` — The pre-flight measures which inputs are missing and then reports a fixed 'provisions NONE' reason *Fix:* Build the durable reason from the measured `missing` list so a partially provisioned install is described truthfully.
- **R3-0172** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands.rs:1559` — conversation_turn_context swallows two database errors into silent fallbacks *Fix:* Return the error; the function already returns Result<_, String>.
- **R3-0173** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands.rs:1605` — hidden_ask_conversation turns a failed read into 'none exists' and creates another *Fix:* Propagate the list error with `?` instead of `.ok()`.
- **R3-0174** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands.rs:2058` — Moving the run to awaiting_approval discards its error *Fix:* Propagate the error (and create the approval and the status change in one transaction).
- **R3-0175** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands.rs:2127` — fail_attempt! swallows both the lock failure and the fail_step_execution result *Fix:* Report a failure to settle the attempt in the emitted error instead of discarding it.
- **R3-0176** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands.rs:2179` — Post-turn error returns in stream_run_step leave the step claimed with no failure recorded *Fix:* Route these returns through fail_attempt! (or an equivalent) so the claim is settled.
- **R3-0177** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands.rs:2551` — delete_automation's changed-while-open check ignores the enabled state the dialog displayed *Fix:* Include `enabled` in the comparison (or compare the whole prompt body).
- **R3-0178** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands.rs:2809` — demonstration_verified_reply prompts the model as Bro and then attributes the reply to the requested agent *Fix:* Resolve the author before building the prompt and name that author in it, as conversation_turn_context does.
- **R3-0179** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/files.rs:431` — write_text overwrites a file the user made read-only *Fix:* Refuse (and report readonly) when the original's permissions are read-only for the owner.
- **R3-0180** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/governance.rs:126` — bounded() has a dead condition and deletes line breaks instead of replacing them *Fix:* Map control characters to a space, then collapse, instead of filtering them out.
- **R3-0181** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/governed_selftest.rs:142` — The self-test waits on the model CLI with no timeout, inside a synchronous Tauri command *Fix:* Bound the wait and kill the child on expiry, reporting BuiltinPlaceholderModelFailed, and run the command off the main thread.
- **R3-0182** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/governed_selftest.rs:182` — Self-test reports trust_state "trusted_verified" for a row that committed as "demonstration_custody" *Fix:* Return the committed label the chain actually wrote (carry it on ProofOutcome) instead of a hardcoded string.
- **R3-0183** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/governed_turn.rs:224` — governed_turn_execute is a synchronous Tauri command that can block for the whole 120 s exchange budget *Fix:* Make it `#[tauri::command(async)]` or run the exchange under spawn_blocking.
- **R3-0184** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/governed_turn.rs:263` — The connect itself has no deadline, so the 'never a wedged command' bound starts only after it returns *Fix:* Connect non-blocking with a deadline, or start the budget before the connect.
- **R3-0185** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/lib.rs:117` — retire_orphaned_anchor treats another OS account's valid machine-wide anchor as an uninstall leftover *Fix:* Decide orphanhood from the anchor manifest's bound trust path, not from the current account's data directory.
- **R3-0186** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-broker/proof/isolation_proof.ps1:240` — Server output is redirected into `$Dir\out\` which the script never creates and the prereqs never mention *Fix:* Create the directory before the first case.
- **R3-0187** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-broker/src/bin/pipe_proof.rs:83` — Security-descriptor setup errors are discarded before the pipe is created *Fix:* Propagate both results as PROOF_ERROR.
- **R3-0188** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-broker/src/bin/spawn_as.rs:221` — SetEntriesInAclW failure is reported from GetLastError instead of its return code *Fix:* Build the error from `rc`.
- **R3-0189** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/WIRING_LIVE_TRUST.md:63` — Runbook order is wrong: step 1 provisions with the executor path that step 2 builds *Fix:* Put the build (including win_provision) first.
- **R3-0190** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/proof/win_live_proof.ps1:33` — The `kit_generated`/`demonstration` custody path of the harness cannot run: win_provision rejects every key but the unheld production pin *Fix:* Either let demonstration provenance provision under the demo root, or remove the unreachable branch and its claims.
- **R3-0191** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/bin/win_provision.rs:194` — store\ is created with a TCB-only inherited DACL, but the supervisor and the broker (non-TCB service accounts) must write into it *Fix:* Have win_provision create store\ and its per-principal subdirectories with explicit descriptors naming exactly the account that writes each.
- **R3-0192** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/bin/win_provision.rs:259` — launcher_executable_sha256 is the hash of a string literal, signed into every lease for a launcher this kit does not have *Fix:* Emit an explicit 'none:no-launcher' marker (as IMAGE_BINDING_IN_PROCESS does) instead of a digest of nothing.
- **R3-0193** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/bin/win_provision.rs:260` — An unreadable or omitted --executor-path is swallowed into a placeholder digest and provisioning reports success *Fix:* Make --executor-path required and exit non-zero when the image cannot be read.
- **R3-0194** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/bin/win_provision.rs:330` — manifest_epoch is the literal 2 and the floor is rebuilt from the manifest just written, so re-provisioning can never be ordered or refused *Fix:* Take the epoch as an argument, load any existing floor.json first and refuse unless the new epoch is strictly higher.
- **R3-0195** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/bin/win_provision.rs:381` — run_id, task_id and requested_at are provisioning-time constants signed into every turn of the deployment *Fix:* Mint run_id/task_id and read requested_at in the driver per turn; keep only workspace/install ids in config.
- **R3-0196** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/bin/win_tcb_pin.rs:181` — Final advice to 'point config.tcb_pin_manifest at it' would invalidate the pin just written *Fix:* Refuse an --out that differs from config.tcb_pin_manifest, or print only the env-var instruction.
- **R3-0197** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/execution.rs:165` — The run-evidence chain hard-codes facts the run did not observe *Fix:* Pass cfg.containment_mode into build_run_evidence and drop launcher_exit / lease-validated, or record only what execute() measured.
- **R3-0198** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/head_sequence.rs:157` — The 'durable' counter never syncs its claim marker, its hint or the directory *Fix:* sync_all the marker and the staged hint, and fsync the directory where the platform allows, before returning the number.
- **R3-0199** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/resolver.rs:164` — persist_floor uses one shared staging name and no fsync — the defect head_sequence.rs names as R-30 *Fix:* Use core's check_and_persist (key_manifest.rs:345 recommends it) or a unique temp name plus sync_all before rename.
- **R3-0200** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/resolver.rs:164` — Windows persist_floor is the fs::write + rename with no sync that core's write_floor_atomically was rewritten to remove *Fix:* Write the signed floor through brops_core::key_manifest::write_floor_atomically.
- **R3-0201** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/servers.rs:138` — Authority pending rows and nonce index are never pruned; supervisor acceptance maps likewise *Fix:* Drop expired pending rows and their nonce keys on each create-pending, as the Python authority does.
- **R3-0202** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/servers.rs:242` — Authority `issue` skips the exact-key shape check every other op applies *Fix:* Add `exact_keys(o, &["op", "pending_challenge_id"])` at the top of issue().
- **R3-0203** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/servers.rs:428` — The evidence-chain size bound is applied after the whole file has been read into memory *Fix:* Check metadata().len() or read through a `take(1<<20 + 1)` reader before allocating.
- **R3-0204** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/servers.rs:1152` — publish treats any existing path as published, so a truncated blob is never repaired *Fix:* Verify the existing file's sha256 equals the handle (or write to a temp name and rename) before reporting it published.
- **R3-0205** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/servers.rs:1446` — Signer checks the evidence by key COUNT only, then indexes with a panicking Map index *Fix:* Check the exact 29 key names (ATTEST_INPUT_FIELDS + decision) and the integer types before building the envelope.
- **R3-0206** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/tcb_floor.rs:334` — Windows floor twin lacks the pin-is-a-digest check the Linux floor added; an entry pinned to "" matches a directory *Fix:* Refuse any WinTcbArtifact whose expected_sha256 is not 64 lowercase hex before comparing, as verify_artifact in core does.
- **R3-0207** `apps/desktop/src/app/store.tsx:97` — A persisted language value is cast, not validated, and an unknown one crashes every translate call *Fix:* Validate the stored value against the Lang union (and Theme likewise) and fall back to the default. **CLOSED 2026-10-08** — REPRODUCED: `brops.lang` = `xx` became the language and `translate` indexes `dicts[lang]`. Both stored values are validated against the union and fall back to `en` / `dark`; a known value is still honoured.
- **R3-0208** `apps/desktop/src/components/Shell.tsx:178` — The gate's boot animation is applied to a hidden empty span, so it never plays *Fix:* Put the ref on the gate's real Mark (or pass a `boot` state to it) and delete the hidden span.
- **R3-0209** `apps/desktop/src/components/Shell.tsx:367` — Window-error dismiss button has a hardcoded English accessible name *Fix:* Use `aria-label={t('action.close')}`. **CLOSED 2026-10-08** — `aria-label={t('action.close')}`; the test was watched red with the literal back.
- **R3-0210** `apps/desktop/src/components/layout.css:32` — Dead topbar `.dot` rule leaks onto the Approvals scope dot *Fix:* Delete the unused `.dot` rule from layout.css (no topbar badge renders it any more).
- **R3-0211** `apps/desktop/src/components/ui.css:214` — Old `.cal-month` label rule now matches Calendar's whole month section *Fix:* Remove the stale `.cal-month` rule from ui.css (or rename the label rule) so the section does not inherit it.
- **R3-0212** `apps/desktop/src/components/ui.css:392` — Beatline draw-on uses a fixed 1200 dash length; a longer line is left with a gap *Fix:* Use `pathLength="1"` on the path with dasharray/offset 1, or clear the dasharray when the animation ends.
- **R3-0213** `apps/desktop/src/components/ui.tsx:105` — ErrorState title is hardcoded English although `t` is in scope *Fix:* Add a `state.loadFailed` key to the three dictionaries and render `t('state.loadFailed')`. **CLOSED 2026-10-08** — `state.loadFailed` in the three dictionaries (212 keys each); watched red in `hy` and `ru` with the literal back.
- **R3-0214** `apps/desktop/src/components/ui.tsx:485` — DataTable labels every activatable row with the table caption *Fix:* Drop the aria-label (the row's cells name it) or build a per-row label.
- **R3-0215** `apps/desktop/src/components/ui.tsx:626` — TileGroup can leave no tile in the tab order *Fix:* Compute the roving index over actionable children only.
- **R3-0216** `apps/desktop/src/domain/enums.ts:9` — ApprovalStatus type lists statuses the database refuses and omits ones it admits *Fix:* Change the type to pending | approved | rejected | consumed | escalated and fix the fixtures that follow.
- **R3-0217** `apps/desktop/src/domain/enums.ts:59` — statusTone has no entry for the decision statuses or `skipped` *Fix:* Add tones for DECISION_STATUSES and STEP_STATUSES and extend the coverage test to them.
- **R3-0218** `apps/desktop/src/domain/statusLabels.ts:10` — Declared statuses `expired`, `open` and `superseded` have no label and render as English in hy/ru *Fix:* Add the three entries with hy/ru values, and a test that every declared status has a label.
- **R3-0219** `apps/desktop/src/features/Activity.tsx:133` — Rate is floored at 1/min, contradicting 'every number shown is DERIVED' *Fix:* Show the real quotient (e.g. '<1' or a per-hour unit) instead of clamping to 1. **CLOSED 2026-10-09** — REPRODUCED: three events across an hour were announced as `About 1 per minute`. The rate is the quotient; below one a minute it reads `<1`, on screen and to a screen reader, and a rate above one is still rounded.
- **R3-0220** `apps/desktop/src/features/Approvals.strings.ts:162` — Two Armenian queue labels mean something different from the English *Fix:* Translate as «Թիրախ» and «Սպասում». **CLOSED 2026-10-08** — «Թիրախ» and «Սպասում»; the two heads read «Ազդեցություն» (impact) and «Ժամկետ» (deadline).
- **R3-0221** `apps/desktop/src/features/Approvals.tsx:355` — After `g`/`d`/`e`, Enter cancels instead of confirming, because Modal puts focus on Cancel *Fix:* Give the confirm button initial focus (autoFocus) or drop the window-level Enter branch and state that Enter acts on the focused button.
- **R3-0222** `apps/desktop/src/features/Approvals.tsx:723` — Every risk level in the queue row is painted with the success tone *Fix:* Choose the class from the risk level, or drop the tone class. **CLOSED 2026-10-08** — REPRODUCED: `high`, `critical` and `medium` rows each carried `q-rev ok`. The tone follows the level — `ok` for low, `no` for high and critical, none for anything else, because `.q-rev` has only those two.
- **R3-0223** `apps/desktop/src/features/Automations.tsx:572` — Every rejected run_automation, including a plain breakdown, is recorded in the history as 'refused by the store' *Fix:* Record a store refusal only when isDenial(message) holds and record other rejections as a failed entry.
- **R3-0224** `apps/desktop/src/features/Automations.tsx:729` — A lane with an empty trigger is announced as 'any event' *Fix:* Use describeTrigger(a.trigger, L).text (or the manual-only string) in the accessible name.
- **R3-0225** `apps/desktop/src/features/Automations.tsx:957` — 'No runs yet' is printed before the run history has been read *Fix:* Render nothing (or a loading note) while runs.loading is true. **CLOSED 2026-10-08** — REPRODUCED with a history read that never answers: the sentence was on screen. It is printed only when the read has answered with no rows; an empty history that did come back still says it.
- **R3-0226** `apps/desktop/src/features/Automations.tsx:1235` — .au-warn references an undeclared token and always falls back to danger red *Fix:* Use var(--warning).
- **R3-0227** `apps/desktop/src/features/Bridge.tsx:39` — Three CSS tokens the panel uses are declared nowhere *Fix:* Use the declared tokens (--line / --raised / --r).
- **R3-0228** `apps/desktop/src/features/Bridge.tsx:165` — A post-send transport failure is rendered as 'No broker allowed or refused this turn' *Fix:* Give broker_transport_failed (and unclassified_transport_failure) the 'no verdict received, the broker may have decided' wording rather than outcomeU…
- **R3-0229** `apps/desktop/src/features/Bridge.tsx:187` — Verdict mirror shows the previous decision's record count under the newly selected decision *Fix:* Pass established(verdicts) (or null while loading) to MirrorRow so a count is never shown for a taskId it was not read for.
- **R3-0230** `apps/desktop/src/features/Bridge.tsx:216` — The panel's accessible name is hard-coded English *Fix:* Use aria-label={L('panelTitle')}. **CLOSED 2026-10-08** — `aria-label={L('panelTitle')}`; red first in `hy`.
- **R3-0231** `apps/desktop/src/features/Bridge.tsx:222` — As a routed page the bridge has no h1 *Fix:* Wrap the routed use in a page header with an h1, keeping h2 for the embedded use.
- **R3-0232** `apps/desktop/src/features/Calendar.tsx:55` — Event kinds are coloured from the task/run status table *Fix:* Give event kinds their own tone map with a neutral default.
- **R3-0233** `apps/desktop/src/features/Calendar.tsx:205` — Run-history times bypass the shared parser and the page locale *Fix:* Format with parseTimestamp(run.ranAt) and the page's locale, with a dash for null. **CLOSED 2026-10-08** — REPRODUCED: an ISO time printed `Invalid Date`. Times go through `parseTimestamp` and the page's locale, and an unreadable one is a dash.
- **R3-0234** `apps/desktop/src/features/Command.tsx:87` — A failed or skipped step is styled as a waiting one *Fix:* Add explicit failed/skipped cases with their own classes. **CLOSED 2026-10-08** — REPRODUCED: `failed` and `skipped` both carried `rs-wait`. They carry `rs-fail` and `rs-skip`, with rules in `aios.css`; contrast and token gates green, real Chromium 434 passed.
- **R3-0235** `apps/desktop/src/features/Conversations.tsx:390` — Mentioned agents do not answer 'in the order named' *Fix:* Sort the matches by their index in the body, or correct the comment. **CLOSED 2026-10-08** — same function: the result is sorted by where each name appears in the message, so the order named is the order answered. Red first.
- **R3-0236** `apps/desktop/src/features/Conversations.tsx:394` — Mention routing uses \b, which is not a boundary after a non-ASCII agent name *Fix:* Use a Unicode-aware lookahead such as `(?![\p{L}\p{N}_])` with the `u` flag. **CLOSED 2026-10-08** — REPRODUCED with the old pattern moved unchanged into a function: `@Գևորգ,`, `@Գևորգ.` and `@Анна!` routed to nobody. `mentionedAgents` ends a name with `(?![\p{L}\p{N}_])` under the `u` flag. Writing it found a second defect before it shipped: an escaped hyphen outside a class is a SyntaxError under `u`, so a name like `code-audit` would have thrown on send; only syntax characters are escaped, and a hyphen, a slash and a space in a name are each tested.
- **R3-0237** `apps/desktop/src/features/Conversations.tsx:566` — The message log is labelled 'Conversations', the same name as the conversation rail *Fix:* Label the log with the conversation title or a 'Messages' string.
- **R3-0238** `apps/desktop/src/features/Conversations.tsx:656` — The '@' mention button carries the 'Reply as' accessible name of the agent select *Fix:* Give the button and the listbox their own 'Mention an agent' label.
- **R3-0239** `apps/desktop/src/features/Conversations.tsx:823` — A failed roster write is 'surfaced' only to the console *Fix:* Show the failure in the workspace (the createError strip already exists) instead of the console.
- **R3-0240** `apps/desktop/src/features/Decisions.tsx:186` — An unreachable engine is announced to screen readers as a sealed chain *Fix:* Announce a distinct string for state === 'unreachable'. **CLOSED 2026-10-08** — by PR #362: the announcement for any read that is not `ok` is `The engine evidence chain could not be read`, and no longer says sealed.
- **R3-0241** `apps/desktop/src/features/Decisions.tsx:458` — 'Select a decision — arrow-navigate the ledger' is shown only when the ledger is empty *Fix:* Render an empty-chamber message for the empty ledger and drop the unreachable select hint.
- **R3-0242** `apps/desktop/src/features/Decisions.tsx:575` — The ledger header counts every row as an 'active' decision *Fix:* Label the figure 'decisions' or count only the non-settled family. **CLOSED 2026-10-09** — the figure is `ledger.length`, and the label is `decisions` in three languages.
- **R3-0243** `apps/desktop/src/features/GroupChat.tsx:592` — Switching rooms keeps the previous room's ask errors on screen *Fix:* Clear askErrors and formError in the roomId effect at line 461. **CLOSED 2026-10-08** — REPRODUCED: two `No position was collected from …` rows were still on screen after the room selector moved. The room effect clears the ask errors and the form error with the delegations.
- **R3-0244** `apps/desktop/src/features/GroupChat.tsx:607` — Handoffs flips from '—' to a number only when a delegation frame arrives, so a measured zero is never shown *Fix:* Track 'an ask has completed for this room' separately and use that for `live`; add the missing ran-with-no-delegation test.
- **R3-0245** `apps/desktop/src/features/Home.tsx:184` — Seeded-event count is taken over all rows while the total it is reported against counts only rows with a parseable time *Fix:* Count seeded over the same filtered set that produces `total`. **CLOSED 2026-10-09** — REPRODUCED: with one of three seeded rows unreadable, the sentence said 3 of them. The count is taken over the rows that are plotted.
- **R3-0246** `apps/desktop/src/features/Home.tsx:227` — The same subtitle string is rendered four times in the hero, including a slot classed `since` *Fix:* Give the eyebrow and the ledger caption their own strings and keep the subtitle once.
- **R3-0247** `apps/desktop/src/features/Integrations.strings.ts:258` — Armenian header eyebrow is half untranslated and names a different thing than en/ru *Fix:* Translate the Armenian half to match 'system map'. **CLOSED 2026-10-08** — «ԻՆՏԵԳՐՈՒՄՆԵՐ · ՀԱՄԱԿԱՐԳԻ ՔԱՐՏԵԶ»; a test holds the Armenian eyebrow to no Latin letters.
- **R3-0248** `apps/desktop/src/features/Integrations.strings.ts:342` — Armenian selectRowHint says something different from en/ru and is formatted differently *Fix:* Rewrite the Armenian string to match the other two. **CLOSED 2026-10-08** — «Ընտրեք տողը՝ դրա ալիքը դիտելու համար։» — a sentence about the channel, like the other two.
- **R3-0249** `apps/desktop/src/features/Integrations.tsx:45` — isGovernanceBlock does not recognise the one governance refusal the backend actually returns, so the `blocked` notice never fires for it *Fix:* Match the approval-required refusal explicitly (or have the backend return a stable machine prefix) and add a test that renders the Blocked notice. **CLOSED 2026-10-08** — by PR #361: `needsApproval` matches `approval: required` and the notice is `blocked`, with the reason. `isGovernanceBlock` itself is unchanged.
- **R3-0250** `apps/desktop/src/features/Integrations.tsx:240` — The `invalid` declare outcome shows a hard-coded English reason in a trilingual page *Fix:* Map the invalid kind to localized strings in Integrations.strings.ts (or disable submit while a field is blank).
- **R3-0251** `apps/desktop/src/features/Knowledge.tsx:737` — `animation: kb-reveal var(--slow) ease both` carries two easings, so the reveal never runs *Fix:* Drop `ease` from the three declarations. **CLOSED 2026-10-08** — all three read `kb-reveal var(--slow) both`. The doubled-easing test of R3-0016 read `.css` files only; it reads the components' own stylesheets now and was red on these three and on a fourth nobody had reported — `lib-reveal var(--slow) var(--enter) both` in `Library.tsx`, fixed the same way. Real Chromium: 434 passed.
- **R3-0252** `apps/desktop/src/features/Library.tsx:311` — An item whose kind is not one of the three frontend constants is counted in the total but in no kind capsule and under no kind filter *Fix:* Validate kind in create_library_item (or add an 'other' capsule and chip) so the capsules sum to the total.
- **R3-0253** `apps/desktop/src/features/Memory.tsx:413` — The 'sealed refs' metric counts memories that contain a sealed reference, not sealed references *Fix:* Count `graph.filter(g => g.sealed).length`, or relabel to 'memories with sealed refs'. **CLOSED 2026-10-09** — the label names memories with sealed references, which is what `blockedIds.size` counts; three languages.
- **R3-0254** `apps/desktop/src/features/Memory.tsx:438` — The page shortcuts ignore modifier keys, so Ctrl/Alt/Meta+N and +E are captured and prevented *Fix:* Return early when any of ctrlKey, metaKey or altKey is set. **CLOSED 2026-10-08** — REPRODUCED: Ctrl+N, Cmd+N, Alt+N and Ctrl+/ were each `preventDefault`ed. The handler returns before reading `e.key` when a modifier is held; the bare key still opens the dialog.
- **R3-0255** `apps/desktop/src/features/Notifications.tsx:99` — A severity outside SEVERITY_ORDER sorts ahead of 'critical' when choosing the lead signal *Fix:* Map -1 to SEVERITY_ORDER.length before comparing.
- **R3-0256** `apps/desktop/src/features/Notifications.tsx:286` — The live region announces '0 signals shown' while the list is loading or the read failed *Fix:* Announce loading and error states in the live region, as Memory.tsx's liveMessage does.
- **R3-0257** `apps/desktop/src/features/Notifications.tsx:295` — A filter that matches nothing shows 'All clear · No signals right now.' even when signals exist *Fix:* Branch on items.length === 0 versus a filtered-empty view and give the latter its own wording.
- **R3-0258** `apps/desktop/src/features/Projects.strings.ts:52` — Armenian empty-state title says 'No active workstreams' where English and Russian say 'No workstreams yet' *Fix:* Change hy to 'Հոսքեր դեռ չկան'.
- **R3-0259** `apps/desktop/src/features/Research.tsx:119` — Save to knowledge has no in-flight guard: a second click turns a successful save into 'The run failed' *Fix:* Add a `saving` state (or busy flag) that disables the Save button and ignores re-entry until the first call settles.
- **R3-0260** `apps/desktop/src/features/Research.tsx:124` — A failed save discards the held result id although the backend puts the answer back for a retry *Fix:* On a save failure keep the run in 'held' (with the error shown) so Save can be pressed again.
- **R3-0261** `apps/desktop/src/features/Research.tsx:131` — 'Enter runs · Esc cancels' keymap is unreachable: the section is not focusable and its only control is disabled while running, so a run cannot be cancelled *Fix:* Render an explicit Cancel button while running (and make the panel focusable if the keymap is to stay), then test Esc and Enter through it.
- **R3-0262** `apps/desktop/src/features/Security.strings.ts:26` — 'Chain read failed' tells the owner to retry the read in the posture section, but nothing on the page can re-run the chain read *Fix:* Add a retry control bound to `chain.reload` in the integrity hero for the `broken` state, or remove the instruction from all three languages.
- **R3-0263** `apps/desktop/src/features/Security.tsx:242` — 'Engine reason:' labels strings the desktop wrote itself when the engine was never reached *Fix:* Use a neutral 'Reason:' label (as Notifications does) unless state is 'blocked' with an engine-supplied reason.
- **R3-0264** `apps/desktop/src/features/Security.tsx:317` — The control-plane digest section prints the evidence-chain read's reason as its own *Fix:* Remove the chain reason from the digest section.
- **R3-0265** `apps/desktop/src/features/Settings.tsx:457` — The System panel's region is named 'Settings', the same name as the page *Fix:* Label it with `L('systemHeading')`.
- **R3-0266** `apps/desktop/src/features/Tasks.tsx:79` — Priority options show the raw enum value while the card shows the localised label *Fix:* Render `priorityLabel(p, lang)` in both selects.
- **R3-0267** `apps/desktop/src/features/Tasks.tsx:223` — The composer's agent is fixed at open time; if the roster arrives later the select shows an agent and the state holds none *Fix:* Sync agentSlug in an effect when it is empty and agents become non-empty, or do not open the modal until the roster has loaded.
- **R3-0268** `apps/desktop/src/features/Tasks.tsx:521` — A failed dependency read is shown as 'no dependencies' on the card and in the edit modal *Fix:* Render `deps.error` distinctly, as homeMetrics.readList does for Home.
- **R3-0269** `apps/desktop/src/features/Tasks.tsx:564` — The card prints the assignee's internal id although the roster is loaded on the same page *Fix:* Resolve the id to `displayName` through the loaded roster, as projectName is resolved.
- **R3-0270** `apps/desktop/src/features/Tasks.tsx:645` — A failed roster read is rendered as 'No agents in the roster' *Fix:* Pass the read state to DispatchModal and show the read error instead of the empty-roster sentence.
- **R3-0271** `apps/desktop/src/features/Tasks.tsx:685` — The page renders its title and subtitle twice, as two `<h1>` elements *Fix:* Keep the PageHeader h1 and make the hero heading an h2 or drop its repeated title.
- **R3-0272** `apps/desktop/src/features/Tasks.tsx:795` — The draft-warning band uses a token that is defined nowhere, so it always falls back to the neutral line colour *Fix:* Use `--warning-rgb`.
- **R3-0273** `apps/desktop/src/features/approvalsAuthority.ts:220` — `canAct`'s grant check can never be false, and the page never reads the command table *Fix:* Either read `granted` from the capability file at test time only and drop it from `canAct`, or route the page's calls through the table.
- **R3-0274** `apps/desktop/src/features/automationsGovernance.ts:193` — parseIntervalMs is not equivalent to the Rust parser it claims to port: `every: +5m` fires in Rust and reads 'unrecognised' here *Fix:* Make one side match the other (reject a sign in Rust, or accept it here) and add a shared fixture both suites read.
- **R3-0275** `apps/desktop/src/features/automationsGovernance.ts:453` — A stored run of a model-reaching verb is classified as an unknown verb, the distinction the module says it must keep *Fix:* Capture the verb from the store message and return `action_reaches_model` when it is in MODEL_REACHING_VERBS.
- **R3-0276** `apps/desktop/src/features/delegationView.tsx:192` — A CLI built-in or unrecognised agent is labelled 'PACK ROLE' on the delegation card *Fix:* Derive the label from `agentOrigin` (tier / pack role / outside the model / unstated) instead of from `tier` alone.
- **R3-0277** `apps/desktop/src/features/integrationsProbe.ts:183` — parseDeclareReply drops `authRef` from the record it returns *Fix:* Copy `authRef` through when it is a string or null.
- **R3-0278** `apps/desktop/src/features/researchLibraryDeleteRefusal.ts:72` — isPolicyRefusal is wider than its docstring: untied prefix match and bare 'denied'/'forbidden' *Fix:* Require `forbidden_command:${command}:` as a prefix and drop the bare 'forbidden|denied' alternatives.
- **R3-0279** `apps/desktop/src/services/agentsDispatch.ts:461` — validateAssignment does not refuse duplicate `inputs`, which the schema forbids *Fix:* Add the same dupes() check for d.inputs.
- **R3-0280** `apps/desktop/src/services/agentsDispatch.ts:475` — Empty-string done_criteria / verification / rollback commands pass pre-flight though the schema requires minLength 1 *Fix:* Refuse any entry that is empty after trim in done_criteria, verification.commands and rollback.commands.
- **R3-0281** `apps/desktop/src/services/agentsDispatch.ts:720` — A refused reply is not correlated with the request; only an accepted one is *Fix:* Apply the client_request_id comparison to refused frames as well.
- **R3-0282** `apps/desktop/src/services/approvalRequest.ts:94` — A `recorded` outcome with no request_id is accepted with an empty id *Fix:* Return blocked when request_id is absent or empty.
- **R3-0283** `apps/desktop/src/services/governedTurn.ts:128` — parseResult accepts frames its own schema refuses (extra properties) *Fix:* Reject any key outside the closed set at frame and message level, with a test per level.
- **R3-0284** `apps/desktop/src/services/governedTurn.ts:139` — created_at_ms accepts a non-integer; the schema says integer *Fix:* Use Number.isSafeInteger(createdAtMs) and drop the redundant `o.message &&`.
- **R3-0285** `apps/desktop/src/test/contrast.ts:118` — Ancestor opacity is applied to the text only, over an undimmed background *Fix:* Composite each opacity-bearing ancestor's whole subtree (text and background) over its backdrop, or document and test the limitation.
- **R3-0286** `apps/desktop/src/theme/aios.css:316` — aios.css `.grid` overrides ui.css `.grid-3`/`.grid-2`, so TileGroup is never three columns *Fix:* Rename one of the two `.grid` utilities or raise `.grid-2/.grid-3` to `.grid.grid-3`.
- **R3-0287** `apps/desktop/src/theme/aios.css:342` — The hover lift on `.surface` cannot show on any surface that also carries `.reveal`/`.rise` *Fix:* Animate `translate`/opacity in `reveal` instead of `transform`, or drop `forwards` and set the end state in the base rule.
- **R3-0288** `apps/desktop/src/theme/aios.css:405` — `pill danger` is still applied and still has no rule; the comment says it was folded away *Fix:* Change Command.tsx to `bad`, or add `.pill.danger` beside `.pill.bad`.
- **R3-0289** `apps/desktop/src/theme/aios.css:2916` — Integrations group headers reserve room for a dot whose colour variable is never set *Fix:* Delete the aios `.creg-gname` rules, or give `--gtc` a fallback.

### test-defect (166)

- **R3-1205** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/audit-signer/tests/anchor_end_to_end.py:707` — The forged-anchor check in case_pin_rewrite passes on any AuditError, with no reason asserted *Fix:* Assert the message names the unresolvable key id `audit-anchor-rogue`.
- **R3-1206** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/audit-signer/tests/anchor_end_to_end.rs:143` — Six tests share one permanent on-disk fixture with no lock; they are only correct under an external --test-threads=1 *Fix:* Take a process-wide Mutex (or a file lock) inside stand_up so a default `cargo test` cannot interleave the cases.
- **R3-1207** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/audit-signer/tests/anchor_end_to_end.rs:629` — The elevated arm of the registration test cannot pass on any machine, and would leave a real service behind *Fix:* Either make the service startable and pass the signer directory in binPath with cleanup, or delete the elevated arm and state that it is unbuilt.
- **R3-1208** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/chain_executor.rs:1295` — The test named for the shipped connector never calls the shipped connector *Fix:* Obtain the connection through LinuxHopConnector::connect (and supervisor_op) and assert the budget on what they return.
- **R3-1209** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/chain_hops.rs:191` — `every_broker_op_parses_its_own_principals_success_reply` cannot fail for any op string *Fix:* Derive the op list from the request builders, or delete the test and rely on the per-hop tests that assert real reply fields.
- **R3-1210** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/ladder_executor.rs:512` — `the_challenge_facts_are_the_prepared_facts` never calls issue_challenge, yet the production doc cites it as the equality *Fix:* Point the doc at `the_create_pending_hop_sends_exactly_the_prepared_facts` and delete or fold the weaker test.
- **R3-1211** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/main.rs:755` — The test module is described as offline with no process or socket, but one test runs sudo on Linux *Fix:* Inject a non-spawning invoker for that loop, or correct the module banner to say one test attempts a spawn.
- **R3-1212** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/main.rs:946` — The task-request door test drives a test-only copy of the Linux wiring, not build_governed_executor *Fix:* Move the three wiring lines into one non-cfg-gated function that build_governed_executor and the test both call.
- **R3-1213** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/manifest_resolver.rs:931` — The rollback test's first resolver is never used and its last-but-one assertion checks the fixture's own input *Fix:* Resolve the epoch-2 manifest first as the control (it must be accepted before epoch 3 is), and delete the tautological assertion.
- **R3-1214** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/preflight.rs:1654` — The config-key drift test scans all of main.rs, comments and test module included, not build_governed_executor *Fix:* Cut the source to the body of build_governed_executor and strip full-line comments before scanning, as tcb_probe.rs's code_only does.
- **R3-1215** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/preflight.rs:1990` — Sixteen negative tests assert only the detail text, never the Status variant — the weakness this file documents at line 1791 *Fix:* Add `assert!(matches!(s, Status::NotMet { .. }))` to each, as the tests at :2123, :2156 and :2178 already do.
- **R3-1216** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/tcb_probe.rs:784` — LinuxFsProbe's digest, mode and symlink handling are tested only by substring-matching this file's own source *Fix:* Add Linux tests that stat a temp file through LinuxFsProbe and assert its sha256, the group-writable flag and an empty digest for a symlink.
- **R3-1217** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/tcb_probe.rs:888` — off_linux_a_broken_or_under_covering_manifest_still_refuses is gated to non-Linux in a crate CI tests only on Linux *Fix:* Add `-p brops-broker` to the windows-latest cargo test job, or delete the test and say the off-Linux arm is unproven.
- **R3-1218** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/tcb_probe.rs:1301` — A test returns early as root without reporting a skip *Fix:* Mark it `#[ignore]` under a root-detecting cfg or print a skip line, so a root run does not count it as a pass.
- **R3-1219** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/tcb_probe.rs:1302` — an_anchor_the_tcb_owner_does_not_own_is_not_floor_pinned returns early, as a pass, when run as root *Fix:* Chown the fixture to a non-root uid when root, or fail unless the gap is declared.
- **R3-1220** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/approval_request.rs:195` — Three of the four 'refusal wearing a record's clothes' keys are guarded by no test *Fix:* Loop the test over each key alone (ok:false plus exactly one worn field) and assert the reason names that key.
- **R3-1221** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/bin/ladder_output_pull.rs:617` — The 'exhaustive' expectation test hand-lists StreamRefusal variants, so a new refusal reason stays unspellable in --expect with the test green *Fix:* Generate StreamRefusal::ALL from one list (as Refusal does in agent_bundle.rs) and iterate it in the test.

- **R3-1282** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/head_sequence.rs:205` — the_counter_is_reconstructed_from_disk_not_from_memory is the first test again plus an exists() check *Fix:* Between calls, assert on the marker files and delete nothing in memory to rely on — e.g. spawn a child process, or assert seq-…​.claim files 1..3 exi…

- **R3-1289** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/servers.rs:2919` — No test can fail if derive_evidence goes back to serde_json::to_vec (the A-05 defect) *Fix:* Add a test whose payload differs between the two encodings (e.g. build with preserve_order in a dev-dependency fixture) or state plainly that A-05 is…

- **R3-1291** `apps/desktop/src/components/CommandPalette.browser.spec.tsx:108` — The scrim-alpha regex reads the blue channel of rgb() as alpha and treats unparsed colours as opaque *Fix:* Parse the colour with the existing computedStyle helpers and read a real alpha channel, defaulting to fail on an unparsed value.

- **R3-1304** `apps/desktop/src/features/Approvals.test.tsx:428` — 'the Approval type really does not carry them' is asserted on the test's own fixture *Fix:* Check the Rust serialiser's field list (or the Approval type via a type-level test) rather than a hand-written fixture.

- **R3-1308** `apps/desktop/src/features/Chat.delegationContract.test.ts:56` — The 'copied character-for-character from task-contract.schema.json' check skips if the schema file moves *Fix:* Require the file under CI; skip only where the engine tree is genuinely not beside the app.
- **R3-1309** `apps/desktop/src/features/Chat.delegationContract.test.ts:57` — The path-grammar parity check skips silently when the schema is not found *Fix:* Fail on a missing schema unless a standalone-checkout flag is set.

- **R3-1330** `apps/desktop/src/features/deleteRefusals.guard.test.ts:24` — SWALLOWED regex is evaded by any `.then` body containing a semicolon, and by a named-but-unused error argument *Fix:* Scan the bounded chain window for the `.catch(` handler and assert its parameter is referenced in its body, instead of a no-semicolon regex.

- **R3-1345** `apps/desktop/src/services/agentsDispatch.boundary.test.ts:383` — 'I-02 mutant' re-implements the test's own `undeclared` filter over a shrunk copy of a test-local set *Fix:* State in the name that this pins the fixture's coverage, or move the register next to the product code so the test reads product data.
- **R3-1346** `apps/desktop/src/services/agentsDispatch.boundary.test.ts:434` — 'I-01 mutant' checks that a test helper reacts to a test-local override *Fix:* Keep it as a self-test of the helper under a name that says so, separate from the tests about the frame.

- **R3-1365** `engine/tests/test_governed_turn_submit_e2e.py:462` — The 'constructs none' half uses a substring search while its sibling reads the syntax tree *Fix:* Use `_called_names("run_supervisor.py")` in both tests.

- **R3-1367** `engine/tests/test_skill_evolution.py:122` — test_the_policy_file_states_the_same_rules asserts four values its own comment says nothing reads *Fix:* Keep the validator call at the end of the test (line 134) and drop the four assertions on unread configuration.
- **R3-1368** `tools/check_c1_tokens.py:122` — root_declarations is called only by tests, so two tests exercise a helper the gate never runs *Fix:* Have main() use root_declarations, or have the test call the path main() uses.
- **R3-1369** `tools/test_check_no_lstrip_prefix.py:235` — Two 'really are fixed' tests assert an exact expression appears in another tool's source *Fix:* Call the two functions with a `./`-prefixed and an `l`-leading path and assert the result.
- **R3-1370** `tools/test_check_push_ready.py:442` — 'the two gates it runs take the arguments it passes' is a substring check of getsource() *Fix:* Invoke `check_bundle_budget.main(["--root", tmp])` and a bare `check_runbook_snippets.main([])` and assert neither exits with a usage error.

### design-contradiction (130)

- **R3-0500** `CLAUDE.md:116` — Canon says the 2026-08-06 audit left 45 surviving findings; the consolidated index for the same head and date records 122 *Fix:* Say 45 is the filed report's count and that the index holds 77 more from two unfiled rounds.
- **R3-0501** `MASTER_EXECUTION_ROADMAP.md:23` — Roadmap says only the main chat seam is governed; the state mirror says all four AI surfaces are *Fix:* Remove the bullet from the roadmap header.
- **R3-0502** `apps/desktop/AUDIT/2026-08-06-independent-audit.md:274` — The live-turn kit is called 'the shipped deployment' in one finding and 'nothing shipped' / 'not deployed' in others *Fix:* Use one term for engine/ci/live (lab kit) throughout and drop 'shipped deployment' from the F-07 title.
- **R3-0503** `apps/desktop/AUDIT/2026-08-06-independent-audit.md:629` — F-15 rests on shipped auto-updates that Part 2 says do not exist *Fix:* Qualify F-15's impact paragraph as latent until an updater exists.
- **R3-0504** `apps/desktop/AUDIT/2026-08-06-independent-audit.md:772` — F-19 gives two line numbers for the same guard in _check_docs_reference_state *Fix:* Use one line number for the guard in both paragraphs.
- **R3-0505** `apps/desktop/AUDIT/2026-08-06-independent-audit.md:1806` — The record gives two different counts and line lists for the setup-python pin in ci.yml *Fix:* Note in one of the two places which count was correct at b91f235.
- **R3-0506** `apps/desktop/AUDIT/2026-08-06-independent-audit.md:2015` — A Phase 8 item is scored by a 'stub' rule that the record's own rubric does not contain *Fix:* State in the rubric line how a stub is scored, or reclassify the item under the level the rubric does define.
- **R3-0507** `apps/desktop/AUDIT/2026-08-06-independent-audit.md:2185` — Challenger note cites the two gate lines one line off from the Part 1 findings *Fix:* Align the line numbers at :2185 to :46 and :42.
- **R3-0508** `apps/desktop/AUDIT/2026-08-06-remediation-audit.md:96` — F-01 verdict says it 'held on five of seven' while listing seven attacks of which only attack (5) failed *Fix:* Correct to 'held on six of seven' in an erratum.
- **R3-0509** `apps/desktop/AUDIT/2026-08-06-remediation-audit.md:602` — R-05 counts the `reply_binding` grep hits as four; its own review and R-03 count five *Fix:* Correct the walkthrough to five hits.
- **R3-0510** `apps/desktop/AUDIT/2026-08-06-remediation-audit.md:1134` — Windows attested evidence is called 29-field and 28-field inside the same finding *Fix:* Correct to the one field count the code at 219c763 had.
- **R3-0511** `apps/desktop/AUDIT/2026-08-06-remediation-audit.md:2453` — R-45 review says 'exactly four cargo test/check lines' and then lists five *Fix:* Say 'four cargo test lines plus one cargo check line'.
- **R3-0512** `apps/desktop/AUDIT/2026-08-06-remediation-audit.md:2797` — §7 coverage gives different line counts for the same snapshot file in different groups (eight files, each off by one) *Fix:* Note that the per-group counts are approximate, or recount once against the 219c763 snapshot.
