- **R3-0608** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/tcb.rs:24` — ROOT_PUBLIC_KEY_HEX is called the ONLY root material compiled in, sixteen lines above a second root *Fix:* Say it is the only PRODUCTION root material and name the demonstration root beside it.
- **R3-0609** `apps/desktop/src/features/Activity.tsx:229` — Header pill says 'RHYTHM · STABLE' for every loaded state while the page claims rhythm is not fabricated *Fix:* Replace with a factual state word (e.g. 'RECORD LOADED') or derive it from the events.
- **R3-0610** `apps/desktop/src/features/Agents.tsx:212` — Dossier states an Owner for every agent although the header says only entity fields are shown *Fix:* Remove the hard-coded Owner fact or label it structural, and update the header comment to describe the Grant block.
- **R3-0611** `apps/desktop/src/features/Analytics.strings.ts:161` — Empty panels blame "the engine" for a read the same file says is local SQLite *Fix:* Say the desktop backend has no such aggregate, not the engine.
- **R3-0612** `apps/desktop/src/features/Automations.strings.ts:284` — "Blocked by the wall" and "request approval" still title local refusals that no wall or approval governs *Fix:* Title the pre-flight panel with `refusalTitle` and the backend denial with a "refused by the backend" string, and remove the approval promise.
- **R3-0613** `apps/desktop/src/features/Automations.strings.ts:320` — Schematic labels say "Governed" on a page whose own copy says no run is governed *Fix:* Label the node and section as the page's local contract check rather than "Governed".
- **R3-0614** `apps/desktop/src/features/Automations.tsx:610` — The 'scheduler sweep' walks conduits the scheduler never polls *Fix:* Restrict the sweep (and the 'Scheduler · sweep' pill) to rows whose parseTrigger kind is 'interval'.
- **R3-0615** `apps/desktop/src/features/Conversations.tsx:519` — Two PARTICIPANTS counts for the same room on the same screen use different formulas *Fix:* Compute the room's participant count in one place and render it in both.
- **R3-0616** `apps/desktop/src/features/GroupChat.tsx:276` — One Group Chat screen shows two 'HANDOFFS' figures with incompatible definitions *Fix:* Give the two figures different names (speaker changes vs delegations) or derive both from one definition.
- **R3-0617** `apps/desktop/src/features/GroupChat.tsx:437` — A room with no roster means 'all agents' to the deck and 'the first two agents' to the thread *Fix:* Extract one 'who answers in this room' function and use it in both places.
- **R3-0618** `apps/desktop/src/features/GroupChat.tsx:718` — GroupChat says the workspace keeps its selection private; Conversations says it publishes it for the deck *Fix:* Rewrite the docstring to state the publish exists and the deck does not consume it, and drop the reference to a report that is not in the tree.
- **R3-0619** `apps/desktop/src/features/Knowledge.tsx:335` — Knowledge reports a refused delete without the permanent-policy classification that Library and Research apply to the same refusal *Fix:* Route Knowledge's (and Memory's) delete rejection through classifyDeleteRefusal and show the same permanent-refusal sentence.
- **R3-0620** `apps/desktop/src/features/Notifications.strings.ts:165` — The gate title was changed so that no state is called 'sealed', but the chain node for the same blocked read still says 'ENGINE · sealed' *Fix:* Rename the node label to 'ENGINE · refused' to match the title vocabulary.
- **R3-0621** `apps/desktop/src/features/Security.strings.ts:9` — The in-flight pill reads 'Verifying…' on a page whose own rule is that this read can never verify anything *Fix:* Label the checking state 'Reading…' in all three languages.
- **R3-0622** `apps/desktop/src/features/delegationSource.ts:26` — Two feature modules call Tauri `invoke` directly although desktop.ts declares itself the single boundary *Fix:* Move both probes behind desktop.ts as the placement note already prescribes.
- **R3-0623** `apps/desktop/src/services/agentsDispatch.ts:62` — UI-quoted "single normative source" is engine/schemas, while contracts/ is declared the source of record *Fix:* Point the constant and both tests at contracts/task-contract.schema.json, or say explicitly that the engine copy is the vendored one.
- **R3-0624** `apps/desktop/src/services/agentsDispatch.ts:337` — buildAssignment says it never repairs an input, then slugs every id *Fix:* Either pass ids through verbatim so validateAssignment reports them, or rewrite the docstring to say ids are normalised with slug().
- **R3-0625** `apps/desktop/src/theme/aios.css:388` — aios pills still paint a translucent tint; the manifest grades an opaque composite over `surface` only *Fix:* Give the aios pills opaque tint tokens, as was done for `--menq-color-*-tint`, and mirror them in the gate.
- **R3-0626** `apps/desktop/src/theme/global.css:16` — The link colour rule is dead and `link-on-surface` grades a colour links do not have *Fix:* Remove the global.css rule and the pair, or decide links are accent-coloured and remove the aios reset.
- **R3-0627** `apps/desktop/src/theme/tokens.css:4` — "No hard-coded colors" against literals in the stylesheet loaded after it *Fix:* Use `--danger`/`--danger-rgb` for the stop button and scope the header's rule to what is enforced.
- **R3-0628** `apps/desktop/src/theme/tokens.ts:274` — `--menq-motion-slow` / `--menq-motion-easing` exist only in an unmounted provider; ui.css always takes its fallback *Fix:* Declare the two variables in tokens.css and remove the TS-only exemption.
- **R3-0629** `docs/design/WAVE_3B_ISOLATED_SIGNER_DESIGN.md:275` — The canonical design note says the broker resolver has no check that the two key ids differ; it has had one since #317 *Fix:* Update the note: distinctness by id and by key bytes is enforced on Linux (broker and both proof drivers); key_usage typing and the Windows kit are w…

### security (33)

- **R3-0855** `apps/desktop/AUDIT/2026-08-06-remediation-audit.md:130` — The record commits the auditing host's account SID, machine\account name and session scratchpad path *Fix:* Redact the SID, host name and session path to placeholders; they add nothing the finding needs.
- **R3-0856** `apps/desktop/SECURITY.md:60` — The environment-variable table omits BROPS_SELFTEST_MODEL_CMD, which makes the Windows app run `cmd /C <value>` from two commands *Fix:* Add the variable and the subprocess it spawns to SECURITY.md's table and its AI-subprocess section.
- **R3-0857** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/main.rs:181` — With no uid argument the broker admits its own uid as the renderer, the collapse the next comment calls wrong *Fix:* Require the allowed-renderer uid argument and refuse to start when it is absent or equals the broker's own uid.
- **R3-0858** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/main.rs:251` — The F-31 inbound deadline is per read, so an allowed-uid peer dribbling bytes still holds the serial accept loop *Fix:* Give read_one_frame a whole-exchange budget re-armed to the remaining time before each read, as UnixHopConn::read_exact_within_budget does.
- **R3-0859** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/broker/src/tcb_probe.rs:38` — The pin manifest's custody check looks at the file only, not at its ancestor directories *Fix:* Run the same ancestor walk over the manifest path that verify_pinned_role runs over each artifact.
- **R3-0860** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/agent_bundle.rs:178` — The 'written by the runtime, never from a prompt' rule is a denylist on a self-declared label; verify() never requires written_by == GRANT_WRITER *Fix:* In verify() refuse any grant whose written_by is not exactly GRANT_WRITER (the label can lower trust, never raise it).
- **R3-0861** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/approval_request.rs:176` — Decision-word refusal scans top-level keys only *Fix:* Walk the reply recursively (or refuse any key outside the known reply field set).
- **R3-0862** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/governed_sidecar.rs:432` — The 'prefix must actually CHANGE principal' check only tests that the account name appears as a token *Fix:* Say plainly that the check is a spelling check and the supervisor's uid gate is the control, or require the token to follow `-u`.
- **R3-0863** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/manifest_authority.rs:95` — Key validity window, revocation and protocol are evaluated once at construction; resolve() has no clock *Fix:* Hold the verified manifest and re-run resolve_production_key with a clock on every resolve, or delete the type.
- **R3-0864** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/tcb_integrity.rs:383` — A pinned path that is not absolute skips the ancestor walk to '/'; nothing refuses it *Fix:* Refuse any artifact whose path does not start with '/' before measuring it.
- **R3-0865** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/windows_broker.rs:284` — verify_image compares digests with no pin-shape check, the defect tcb_integrity fixed *Fix:* Require a 64-lowercase-hex pin in verify_image before comparing.
- **R3-0866** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/proof/src/bin/governed_recorder.rs:802` — Launcher image is hashed on one descriptor and then exec'd by path; its ancestors are never checked *Fix:* Keep the measured launcher fd open and `fexecve` it (or check the launcher/executor/lease ancestors with the same rule as the policy).
- **R3-0867** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/provision/src/audit_signer.rs:280` — WRITE_ACCESS_BITS is called 'every bit that lets a holder change the object' but omits FILE_DELETE_CHILD and FILE_WRITE_EA on a directory *Fix:* Add FILE_DELETE_CHILD (and FILE_WRITE_EA) to the mask used for the ledger directory, as a superset of pipe_acl's.
- **R3-0868** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/provision/src/audit_signer.rs:725` — verify_key_custody is a denylist: a read ACE for any group outside the 12 WORLD_SIDS passes *Fix:* Refuse any allow ACE whose SID is not SYSTEM, Administrators or the signer.
- **R3-0869** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/provision/src/audit_signer.rs:1953` — dacl_facts drops every ACE that is not type ACCESS_ALLOWED, including allow-callback and allow-object ACEs *Fix:* Skip only the known deny types and return AclNotEnumerable for any other ACE type.
- **R3-0870** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/provision/src/audit_signer.rs:2244` — key_readback_proof treats every Unmeasurable as 'denied the descriptor', but dacl_facts returns it for any error code *Fix:* Classify the GetNamedSecurityInfoW code as classify_failed_key_open does, and accept only ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED as the property.
- **R3-0871** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/provision/src/posix_install.rs:604` — The uid the installer and the verify report rest on is the owner of a file created in $TMPDIR, not the kernel's euid *Fix:* Read the uid from /proc/self (already the test oracle) or pin the probe directory instead of trusting TMPDIR.
- **R3-0872** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/ai.rs:807` — Sandbox self-heal accepts a directory it did not create *Fix:* On AlreadyExists verify owner, mode 0700 and the marker (via symlink_metadata) or allocate a fresh sandbox name.
- **R3-0873** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/ai.rs:1257` — Root contracts/ (the source of the cross-half schemas) is unprotected while its engine copies are protected *Fix:* Add contracts to BRO_PROTECTED_PATHS.
- **R3-0874** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/ai.rs:1257` — The root-run .deb postinst is not protected *Fix:* Add apps/desktop/src-tauri/deb to BRO_PROTECTED_PATHS.
- **R3-0875** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/ai.rs:1257` — The test suites and engine CI kits that prove the trust code are writable by a turn *Fix:* Add engine/tests, engine/ci and apps/desktop/src-tauri/tests to the list, or state in the doc that tests are deliberately excluded.
- **R3-0876** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/ai.rs:1632` — New files under a skip-named directory inside a protected subtree are neither reported nor seen *Fix:* Skip those names only where a build really writes them, or report a newly created skip-named directory.
- **R3-0877** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands.rs:667` — The renderer can create the 'system-owned' ask conversation kind, and the hidden lookup adopts the newest one *Fix:* Refuse kind 'ask' in the create_conversation command; only hidden_ask_conversation may create it.
- **R3-0878** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/commands.rs:849` — post_user_message lets the renderer choose the author name, which becomes the audit actor id *Fix:* Fix the user author server-side (as create_decision does with Actor::local_operator) or validate it against a non-agent allowlist.
- **R3-0879** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/files.rs:63` — The home-directory clamp compares against HOME only when both HOME and USERPROFILE are set *Fix:* Check the root against every candidate home (HOME and USERPROFILE), not the first one found.
- **R3-0880** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-broker/proof/isolation_proof.ps1:40` — Default credentials file (plaintext service-account passwords) lives in the directory the header requires to be world-readable *Fix:* Default the creds path outside the staged directory and refuse to run if the file is readable by non-administrators.
- **R3-0881** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/bin/win_signer.rs:27` — The §2.5 floor measures the paths in the pin manifest, never the config the bin loaded or the image that is running *Fix:* Pass the canonical --config path and std::env::current_exe() to the floor and refuse unless they equal the manifest's kit.config and the component's…
- **R3-0882** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/bin/win_tcb_pin.rs:119` — The pinned executor is <bin-dir>\win_executor.exe, not the config's executor_path that the driver actually spawns *Fix:* Have win_tcb_pin read executor_path from config.json and pin that path (refusing if empty), instead of assuming the bin-dir name.
- **R3-0883** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/provision_custody.rs:509` — resolve_trustee_sid fetches the account type and ignores it, so a group name becomes a seed reader *Fix:* Refuse unless use_ is SidTypeUser (or a well-known service SID).
- **R3-0884** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/servers.rs:1451` — Windows signer never checks attestation_protocol or the attestation key set *Fix:* Require exact_keys(attestation, [attestation_protocol, supervisor_key_id, sig]) and compare the protocol, then fix the fixture.
- **R3-0885** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/tcb.rs:68` — The anti-rollback floor is signed with a private seed published in source *Fix:* Treat the signature as a checksum in name and docs, or replace it with a key the deployment directory writer cannot derive (TPM/monotonic counter).
- **R3-0886** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/tcb_floor.rs:38` — The Windows TCB probe measures by path, so the measured object can be swapped before use *Fix:* Open one handle with FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT and take owner, DACL and digest from that handle.
- **R3-0887** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/win-live/src/tcb_floor.rs:250` — Manifest custody checks the pin-manifest file only, not its ancestor directories *Fix:* Apply the same ancestor walk to the manifest path before loading it.

### other (8)

- **R3-0847** `apps/desktop/AUDIT/2026-08-06-consolidated-index.md:19` — 'Yield declined 45 -> 49 -> 28' — the first step is an increase *Fix:* Note in the record that yield rose in round 2 and fell only in round 3.
- **R3-0848** `apps/desktop/AUDIT/2026-08-06-remediation-audit.md:2617` — §5.4 files a finding that is in no register: no R-id, no severity, not in the 45 *Fix:* Give the §5.4 finding an identifier and severity in an erratum so it can be tracked like R-01..R-45.
- **R3-0849** `apps/desktop/SECURITY.md:62` — The configuration table omits environment variables that name an executable the host runs *Fix:* Add the interpreter, sidecar, self-test command, token cap and broker config variables to the table.
- **R3-0850** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/production_trust.rs:114` — resolve_trust_state's doc comment is attached to verifying_key_hex; resolve_trust_state itself has no doc *Fix:* Move lines 114-126 to directly above resolve_trust_state.
- **R3-0851** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/core/src/repo.rs:4730` — First launch seeds fabricated agents, projects, runs, approvals, decisions and integrations; only Activity and Home mark seeded rows *Fix:* Carry a seed marker on every seeded entity and label it on each page, or stop seeding in release builds.
- **R3-0852** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/launcher/src/main.rs:235` — Doc comment for the stat mode masks is attached to the TCB owner constant, and the Lease section banner is spliced mid-sentence *Fix:* Move the mask sentence down to line 256 and put the PARTIAL paragraph after the sentence it interrupts.
- **R3-0853** `apps/desktop/src-tauri/launcher/src/main.rs:577` — TODO: store-input ceiling is a hard-coded 8 MiB, not sourced from the lease/store policy *Fix:* Plumb the field or record the constant as the decided value.
- **R3-0854** `apps/desktop/src/features/delegation.ts:105` — Orphaned doc comment: the ToolsSource description is stacked on top of the AgentOrigin one *Fix:* Move the one-line comment down to `ToolsSource`.
